  "plan": "# Plan: Implement FocusScope agnostic core (task #212)\n\n## Context\n\nTask #212 (epic #10) implements the **framework-agnostic FocusScope core** — a stateful utility that constrains keyboard Tab focus within a container, enabling focus trapping for `Dialog`, `Drawer`, `Popover`, and other overlay components. The spec at `spec/components/utility/focus-scope.md` defines its contract; the issue lists 10 tests and the acceptance criteria.\n\nWhy this matters: FocusScope is critical infrastructure. Several already-implemented components (Dialog, Popover) currently model focus-trap behavior inline via per-component `Effect::FocusInitial` / `Effect::FocusFirstTabbable` markers. Landing FocusScope's agnostic core gives those overlays a reusable trap primitive and unblocks downstream work (Drawer, ComboBox listbox, etc.).\n\nThe spec at §1.6 contains drift: its transition closures call `use_platform_effects()` synchronously, but that function lives in adapter crates (`ars-leptos`, `ars-dioxus`) — the agnostic core cannot reach it. Dialog and Popover have already established the correct pattern: a typed `Effect` enum with `PendingEffect::named(Effect::Foo)` markers that adapters dispatch on (`crates/ars-components/src/overlay/dialog/mod.rs:549`, `crates/ars-components/src/overlay/popover/mod.rs:96`). This task adopts that pattern for FocusScope and updates the spec to match.\n\n## Scope\n\nIn scope (this PR):\n- New module `crates/ars-components/src/utility/focus_scope/mod.rs` with state machine, props, context, events, effect-intent enum, connect API, snapshot tests, unit tests, and proptest invariants.\n- Spec sync to `spec/components/utility/focus-scope.md` resolving the named-effect drift in §1.6, §1.6.1, §1.7.\n- New entry in `crates/ars-components/src/utility/mod.rs` exposing the module.\n\nOut of scope (deferred to separate tasks):\n- Leptos and Dioxus adapter components — the per-framework integrations are tracked under epic #10's adapter tasks.\n- DOM-level focus query, sentinel rendering, keydown handlers — adapter responsibilities.\n- `FocusRestorationStack` for nested scopes (spec §8) — adapter-scoped client-only state.\n- `audit_focus_on_orientation_change` (spec §1.6.1) — adapter-scoped.\n- Extending `EffectMetadata` to carry focus targets — `ctx.saved_focus` is sufficient.\n- Adding `has_layout` to the `PlatformEffects` trait — `can_restore_focus` already encapsulates the layout check.\n\n## Module Structure\n\nSingle-file module at `crates/ars-components/src/utility/focus_scope/mod.rs`, matching the convention of `live_region/mod.rs` and `dismissable/mod.rs` (no child files). Tests live inline under `#[cfg(test)] mod tests { ... }`; snapshot fixtures land in `crates/ars-components/src/utility/focus_scope/snapshots/`.\n\nRegister the module in `crates/ars-components/src/utility/mod.rs` and re-export from the crate root if utility submodules are surfaced there.\n\n## Public API surface\n\nAll types are `pub` and documented (workspace `missing_docs` is hard-enforced).\n\n```rust\n// State / Event / Context / Props\npub enum State { Inactive, Active { trapped: bool } }\npub enum Event {\n    Activate { trapped: bool, saved_focus_id: Option<String> },\n    Deactivate { restore_focus: bool },\n    TrapFocus,\n    ReleaseTrap,\n    RestoreFocus,\n    FocusFirst,\n    FocusLast,\n}\npub struct Context { pub saved_focus: Option<String>, pub container_id: Option<String> }\npub struct Props {\n    pub id: String,\n    pub trapped: bool,\n    pub contain: bool,\n    pub auto_focus: bool,\n    pub restore_focus: bool,\n}\n// Default: id=\"\", trapped=false, contain=false, auto_focus=true, restore_focus=true\n\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct Messages;\nimpl ComponentMessages for Messages {}\n\n// Typed effect intents (named-marker pattern; adapter dispatches)\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub enum Effect {\n    FocusTrapListener, // setup: attach Tab keydown handler + sentinels; cleanup: tear down handler\n    FocusFirst,        // setup: focus first tabbable in container (or fall back to container)\n    FocusLast,         // setup: focus last tabbable in container (or fall back to container)\n    RestoreFocus,      // setup: read ctx.saved_focus, restore with fallback chain (see §7)\n}\n\n#[derive(ComponentPart)]\n#[scope = \"focus-scope\"]\npub enum Part { Container }\n\npub struct Machine;\npub struct Api<'a> { state: &'a State, ctx: &'a Context, props: &'a Props, send: &'a dyn Fn(Event) }\npub struct FocusNavigationOptions { pub wrap: bool, pub from: Option<String>, pub tabbable: bool, pub accept: Option<Rc<dyn Fn(&str) -> bool>> }\n```\n\n## State machine transitions\n\n```text\n(Inactive, Activate { trapped, saved_focus_id })\n  → Active { trapped: trapped || props.contain }\n  apply: ctx.saved_focus = saved_focus_id\n  effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusTrapListener)\n  then(Event::FocusFirst) if props.auto_focus\n\n(Active { .. }, Deactivate { restore_focus })\n  → Inactive\n  cancel_effect: Effect::FocusTrapListener  (adapter cleanup tears down keydown handler)\n  if restore_focus:\n    effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::RestoreFocus)  (adapter reads ctx.saved_focus)\n  else:\n    apply: ctx.saved_focus = None\n\n(Active { trapped: false }, TrapFocus) → Active { trapped: true }\n(Active { trapped: true },  ReleaseTrap) → Active { trapped: false }\n\n(Inactive, RestoreFocus)\n  → Inactive (no state change)\n  effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::RestoreFocus)\n\n(Active { .. }, RestoreFocus) → None  (ignored — only meaningful after Deactivate)\n\n(Active { .. }, FocusFirst) → Active (no state change), effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusFirst)\n(Active { .. }, FocusLast)  → Active (no state change), effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusLast)\n(Inactive, FocusFirst | FocusLast) → None\n\n_ → None\n```\n\n`Machine::initial_effects` keeps the trait default (returns empty `Vec`) — the spec has no `default_active` prop so `init` always returns `State::Inactive`. No initial open-lifecycle needs to fire.\n\n## Connect API (`container_attrs`)\n\n```rust\nlet mut attrs = AttrMap::new();\nlet [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Container.data_attrs();\nattrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);\nif self.is_active() {\n    attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-active\"), true);\n    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::TabIndex, \"-1\");\n}\nif self.is_trapped() {\n    attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data(\"ars-trapped\"), true);\n}\nattrs\n```\n\n`Api::is_active()` → `matches!(state, State::Active { .. })`. `Api::is_trapped()` → `matches!(state, State::Active { trapped: true })`. Imperative methods `activate(trapped, saved_focus_id)`, `deactivate(restore_focus)`, `focus_first()`, `focus_last()` all send their corresponding `Event`. `focus_next(_)` and `focus_previous(_)` return `false` (matches spec stub — the adapter exposes platform-aware variants on its own API surface).\n\n`get_tabbable_elements()` is **removed** from the core Api — synchronous DOM queries cannot run in the agnostic core (the issue's \"Element/ref handling note\" is explicit). Adapters call `PlatformEffects::tabbable_element_ids` directly when consumers need this.\n\n**Issue/spec attribute typo:** Issue #212 test 9 says `data-ars-focus-trapped`, but spec §2 says `data-ars-trapped`. The spec is authoritative — implement `data-ars-trapped`. Cross-reference both names in the PR description.\n\n## Effect contract (adapter handles)\n\n| Effect              | When emitted                                              | Adapter contract                                                                                                              |\n| ------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `FocusTrapListener` | `Inactive → Active` (Activate)                            | `platform.attach_focus_trap(container_id, on_escape) -> CleanupFn`. Wire the returned cleanup as the effect's cleanup.        |\n| `FocusFirst`        | `Active + FocusFirst` (also on Activate when auto_focus)  | `platform.focus_first_tabbable(container_id)`. Fall back to focusing the container element when no tabbables exist.           |\n| `FocusLast`         | `Active + FocusLast`                                      | `platform.focus_last_tabbable(container_id)`. Fall back to the container.                                                     |\n| `RestoreFocus`      | `Active → Inactive` (Deactivate) or explicit RestoreFocus | Read `service.context().saved_focus`. If `Some(id)`, run the §7 fallback chain via `PlatformEffects` methods. Adapters MAY clear via a follow-up event but the core does not require it (next `Activate` overwrites). |\n\n`cancel_effect(Effect::FocusTrapListener)` invokes the cleanup captured at install time. No separate `Effect::FocusTrapRelease` variant is needed.\n\n## Spec sync (lands in this PR)\n\nUpdate `spec/components/utility/focus-scope.md`:\n\n1. **§1.2 Events table** — keep all seven events. No change.\n2. **§1.6 Full Machine Implementation**:\n   - Add a `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)] pub enum Effect { FocusTrapListener, FocusFirst, FocusLast, RestoreFocus }` block before the `impl Machine`.\n   - Add `type Effect = Effect;` to the `Machine` impl.\n   - Replace every `.with_named_effect(\"string\", |ctx, _props, send| { use_platform_effects(); ... })` with `.with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::Foo))`.\n   - On `Deactivate`, add `.cancel_effect(Effect::FocusTrapListener)`.\n   - Rewrite the `(Inactive, RestoreFocus)` branch to use `TransitionPlan::new().with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::RestoreFocus))` — drop the inline `restore_focus_safely` call and the `ctx.saved_focus = None` clear (next `Activate` overwrites; clarify in narrative).\n3. **§1.6.1 Focus Restoration Safety** — convert the `restore_focus_safely` Rust function to a narrative bullet list (\"Adapter contract for `Effect::RestoreFocus`\") that names the six DOM checks and fallback order. Remove `audit_focus_on_orientation_change` from the file (out of agnostic-core scope; if normative anywhere, it lives in `spec/foundation/11-dom-utilities.md` §3.2).\n4. **§1.7 Api** — change `focus_first()` / `focus_last()` to send `Event::FocusFirst` / `Event::FocusLast`. Delete `get_tabbable_elements()` (move responsibility into adapter API). Keep `focus_next` / `focus_previous` as `-> bool` stubs that return `false`, with a note that adapters override via their own API for components needing programmatic sequential focus.\n5. **§1.7 add a new \"Effect Contract\" subsection** documenting the four variants and their adapter responsibilities (mirroring the table above).\n\n`spec/foundation/11-dom-utilities.md` §3 needs no changes — its focus utilities already document the platform-level helpers adapters use.\n\n## Tests (TDD order — write before implementation)\n\nAll tests are inline in `mod.rs` under `#[cfg(test)] mod tests`. # Plan: Implement FocusScope agnostic core (task #212)

## Context

Task #212 (epic #10) implements the **framework-agnostic FocusScope core** — a stateful utility that constrains keyboard Tab focus within a container, enabling focus trapping for `Dialog`, `Drawer`, `Popover`, and other overlay components. The spec at `spec/components/utility/focus-scope.md` defines its contract; the issue lists 10 tests and the acceptance criteria.

Why this matters: FocusScope is critical infrastructure. Several already-implemented components (Dialog, Popover) currently model focus-trap behavior inline via per-component `Effect::FocusInitial` / `Effect::FocusFirstTabbable` markers. Landing FocusScope's agnostic core gives those overlays a reusable trap primitive and unblocks downstream work (Drawer, ComboBox listbox, etc.).

The spec at §1.6 contains drift: its transition closures call `use_platform_effects()` synchronously, but that function lives in adapter crates (`ars-leptos`, `ars-dioxus`) — the agnostic core cannot reach it. Dialog and Popover have already established the correct pattern: a typed `Effect` enum with `PendingEffect::named(Effect::Foo)` markers that adapters dispatch on (`crates/ars-components/src/overlay/dialog/mod.rs:549`, `crates/ars-components/src/overlay/popover/mod.rs:96`). This task adopts that pattern for FocusScope and updates the spec to match.

## Scope

In scope (this PR):
- New module `crates/ars-components/src/utility/focus_scope/mod.rs` with state machine, props, context, events, effect-intent enum, connect API, snapshot tests, unit tests, and proptest invariants.
- Spec sync to `spec/components/utility/focus-scope.md` resolving the named-effect drift in §1.6, §1.6.1, §1.7.
- New entry in `crates/ars-components/src/utility/mod.rs` exposing the module.

Out of scope (deferred to separate tasks):
- Leptos and Dioxus adapter components — the per-framework integrations are tracked under epic #10's adapter tasks.
- DOM-level focus query, sentinel rendering, keydown handlers — adapter responsibilities.
- `FocusRestorationStack` for nested scopes (spec §8) — adapter-scoped client-only state.
- `audit_focus_on_orientation_change` (spec §1.6.1) — adapter-scoped.
- Extending `EffectMetadata` to carry focus targets — `ctx.saved_focus` is sufficient.
- Adding `has_layout` to the `PlatformEffects` trait — `can_restore_focus` already encapsulates the layout check.

## Module Structure

Single-file module at `crates/ars-components/src/utility/focus_scope/mod.rs`, matching the convention of `live_region/mod.rs` and `dismissable/mod.rs` (no child files). Tests live inline under `#[cfg(test)] mod tests { ... }`; snapshot fixtures land in `crates/ars-components/src/utility/focus_scope/snapshots/`.

Register the module in `crates/ars-components/src/utility/mod.rs` and re-export from the crate root if utility submodules are surfaced there.

## Public API surface

All types are `pub` and documented (workspace `missing_docs` is hard-enforced).

```rust
// State / Event / Context / Props
pub enum State { Inactive, Active { trapped: bool } }
pub enum Event {
    Activate { trapped: bool, saved_focus_id: Option<String> },
    Deactivate { restore_focus: bool },
    TrapFocus,
    ReleaseTrap,
    RestoreFocus,
    FocusFirst,
    FocusLast,
}
pub struct Context { pub saved_focus: Option<String>, pub container_id: Option<String> }
pub struct Props {
    pub id: String,
    pub trapped: bool,
    pub contain: bool,
    pub auto_focus: bool,
    pub restore_focus: bool,
}
// Default: id="", trapped=false, contain=false, auto_focus=true, restore_focus=true

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Messages;
impl ComponentMessages for Messages {}

// Typed effect intents (named-marker pattern; adapter dispatches)
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum Effect {
    FocusTrapListener, // setup: attach Tab keydown handler + sentinels; cleanup: tear down handler
    FocusFirst,        // setup: focus first tabbable in container (or fall back to container)
    FocusLast,         // setup: focus last tabbable in container (or fall back to container)
    RestoreFocus,      // setup: read ctx.saved_focus, restore with fallback chain (see §7)
}

#[derive(ComponentPart)]
#[scope = "focus-scope"]
pub enum Part { Container }

pub struct Machine;
pub struct Api<'a> { state: &'a State, ctx: &'a Context, props: &'a Props, send: &'a dyn Fn(Event) }
pub struct FocusNavigationOptions { pub wrap: bool, pub from: Option<String>, pub tabbable: bool, pub accept: Option<Rc<dyn Fn(&str) -> bool>> }
```

## State machine transitions

```text
(Inactive, Activate { trapped, saved_focus_id })
  → Active { trapped: trapped || props.contain }
  apply: ctx.saved_focus = saved_focus_id
  effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusTrapListener)
  then(Event::FocusFirst) if props.auto_focus

(Active { .. }, Deactivate { restore_focus })
  → Inactive
  cancel_effect: Effect::FocusTrapListener  (adapter cleanup tears down keydown handler)
  if restore_focus:
    effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::RestoreFocus)  (adapter reads ctx.saved_focus)
  else:
    apply: ctx.saved_focus = None

(Active { trapped: false }, TrapFocus) → Active { trapped: true }
(Active { trapped: true },  ReleaseTrap) → Active { trapped: false }

(Inactive, RestoreFocus)
  → Inactive (no state change)
  effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::RestoreFocus)

(Active { .. }, RestoreFocus) → None  (ignored — only meaningful after Deactivate)

(Active { .. }, FocusFirst) → Active (no state change), effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusFirst)
(Active { .. }, FocusLast)  → Active (no state change), effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusLast)
(Inactive, FocusFirst | FocusLast) → None

_ → None
```

`Machine::initial_effects` keeps the trait default (returns empty `Vec`) — the spec has no `default_active` prop so `init` always returns `State::Inactive`. No initial open-lifecycle needs to fire.

## Connect API (`container_attrs`)

```rust
let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Container.data_attrs();
attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val).set(part_attr, part_val);
if self.is_active() {
    attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-active"), true);
    attrs.set(HtmlAttr::TabIndex, "-1");
}
if self.is_trapped() {
    attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-trapped"), true);
}
attrs
```

`Api::is_active()` → `matches!(state, State::Active { .. })`. `Api::is_trapped()` → `matches!(state, State::Active { trapped: true })`. Imperative methods `activate(trapped, saved_focus_id)`, `deactivate(restore_focus)`, `focus_first()`, `focus_last()` all send their corresponding `Event`. `focus_next(_)` and `focus_previous(_)` return `false` (matches spec stub — the adapter exposes platform-aware variants on its own API surface).

`get_tabbable_elements()` is **removed** from the core Api — synchronous DOM queries cannot run in the agnostic core (the issue's "Element/ref handling note" is explicit). Adapters call `PlatformEffects::tabbable_element_ids` directly when consumers need this.

**Issue/spec attribute typo:** Issue #212 test 9 says `data-ars-focus-trapped`, but spec §2 says `data-ars-trapped`. The spec is authoritative — implement `data-ars-trapped`. Cross-reference both names in the PR description.

## Effect contract (adapter handles)

| Effect              | When emitted                                              | Adapter contract                                                                                                              |
| ------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `FocusTrapListener` | `Inactive → Active` (Activate)                            | `platform.attach_focus_trap(container_id, on_escape) -> CleanupFn`. Wire the returned cleanup as the effect's cleanup.        |
| `FocusFirst`        | `Active + FocusFirst` (also on Activate when auto_focus)  | `platform.focus_first_tabbable(container_id)`. Fall back to focusing the container element when no tabbables exist.           |
| `FocusLast`         | `Active + FocusLast`                                      | `platform.focus_last_tabbable(container_id)`. Fall back to the container.                                                     |
| `RestoreFocus`      | `Active → Inactive` (Deactivate) or explicit RestoreFocus | Read `service.context().saved_focus`. If `Some(id)`, run the §7 fallback chain via `PlatformEffects` methods. Adapters MAY clear via a follow-up event but the core does not require it (next `Activate` overwrites). |

`cancel_effect(Effect::FocusTrapListener)` invokes the cleanup captured at install time. No separate `Effect::FocusTrapRelease` variant is needed.

## Spec sync (lands in this PR)

Update `spec/components/utility/focus-scope.md`:

1. **§1.2 Events table** — keep all seven events. No change.
2. **§1.6 Full Machine Implementation**:
   - Add a `#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)] pub enum Effect { FocusTrapListener, FocusFirst, FocusLast, RestoreFocus }` block before the `impl Machine`.
   - Add `type Effect = Effect;` to the `Machine` impl.
   - Replace every `.with_named_effect("string", |ctx, _props, send| { use_platform_effects(); ... })` with `.with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::Foo))`.
   - On `Deactivate`, add `.cancel_effect(Effect::FocusTrapListener)`.
   - Rewrite the `(Inactive, RestoreFocus)` branch to use `TransitionPlan::new().with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::RestoreFocus))` — drop the inline `restore_focus_safely` call and the `ctx.saved_focus = None` clear (next `Activate` overwrites; clarify in narrative).
3. **§1.6.1 Focus Restoration Safety** — convert the `restore_focus_safely` Rust function to a narrative bullet list ("Adapter contract for `Effect::RestoreFocus`") that names the six DOM checks and fallback order. Remove `audit_focus_on_orientation_change` from the file (out of agnostic-core scope; if normative anywhere, it lives in `spec/foundation/11-dom-utilities.md` §3.2).
4. **§1.7 Api** — change `focus_first()` / `focus_last()` to send `Event::FocusFirst` / `Event::FocusLast`. Delete `get_tabbable_elements()` (move responsibility into adapter API). Keep `focus_next` / `focus_previous` as `-> bool` stubs that return `false`, with a note that adapters override via their own API for components needing programmatic sequential focus.
5. **§1.7 add a new "Effect Contract" subsection** documenting the four variants and their adapter responsibilities (mirroring the table above).

`spec/foundation/11-dom-utilities.md` §3 needs no changes — its focus utilities already document the platform-level helpers adapters use.

After implementation, run:

```bash
# 1. TDD loop — fast iteration on the new module:

# 2. Whole-crate sanity:

# 3. Snapshot review (commit only after eyeballing the .snap output):

cargo xci --profile fast
```

Acceptance is reached when:

- All 35+ unit/connect-API tests pass.
- 3 (or more, up to the snapshot-count budget) `.snap` fixtures land, all marked reviewed (`snapshot-reviewed` label on the PR).
- All 3 proptest invariants pass at 1,000 cases (default) and 10,000 cases (nightly `extended-proptest`).

- `cargo mutants` shows zero `MISSED` after triage.
- Spec edits compile under `cargo xtask ci spec-compile-snippets`.
- `cargo xci --profile fast` passes locally before push.
- After push, the `post-implementation-audit` skill is invoked, all findings land in this PR, and `waiting-for-codex-review` is invoked until Codex returns 👍.

/// `as_child` pattern primitives.

pub mod error_boundary;

/// Fieldset machine.

1	//! Spec-conformance tests for `crates/ars-components/src/utility/*`.
2	//!
3	//! Each test pulls the expected anatomy from the corresponding component
4	//! spec's §2 anatomy table and asserts the impl's `Part` enum matches.
5	
6	#[cfg(feature = "i18n")]
7	use ars_components::utility::highlight;
8	use ars_components::utility::{download_trigger, group, live_region, swap, toggle};
9	
10	use super::helper::assert_anatomy;
11	
12	#[test]
13	fn group_anatomy_matches_spec() {
14	    // Group's anatomy table (spec §2) declares a single row: `Root`.
15	    // Children are not parts — they are an unenumerated subtree that
16	    // inherits state through `GroupContext`, so the `Part` enum stays
17	    // single-variant.
18	    assert_anatomy("group", &[(group::Part::Root, "root")]);
19	}
20	
21	#[test]
22	fn download_trigger_anatomy_matches_spec() {
23	    // DownloadTrigger anatomy table (spec §2): single `Root` row (`<a>`).
24	    assert_anatomy(
25	        "download-trigger",
26	        &[(download_trigger::Part::Root, "root")],
27	    );
28	}
29	
30	#[test]
31	fn toggle_anatomy_matches_spec() {
32	    assert_anatomy(
33	        "toggle",
34	        &[
35	            (toggle::Part::Root, "root"),
36	            (toggle::Part::Indicator, "indicator"),
37	        ],
38	    );
39	}
40	
41	#[test]
42	fn swap_anatomy_matches_spec() {
43	    assert_anatomy(
44	        "swap",
45	        &[
46	            (swap::Part::Root, "root"),
47	            (swap::Part::OnContent, "on-content"),
48	            (swap::Part::OffContent, "off-content"),
49	        ],
50	    );
51	}
52	
53	#[test]
54	fn live_region_anatomy_matches_spec() {
55	    assert_anatomy("live-region", &[(live_region::Part::Root, "root")]);
56	}
57	
58	#[cfg(feature = "i18n")]
59	#[test]
60	fn highlight_anatomy_matches_spec() {
61	    // Highlight's anatomy table (spec §2) lists two rows: `Root` and
62	    // `Chunk`. Only `Root` is a static `Part` enum variant — `Chunk` is
63	    // a parametric anatomy slot driven by a runtime boolean and served
64	    // by `Api::chunk_attrs(highlighted)`, per the convention documented
65	    // in `foundation/10-component-spec-template.md` §4.2.
66	    assert_anatomy("highlight", &[(highlight::Part::Root, "root")]);
67	}
68

320	    #[test]
321	    #[ignore = "proptest — nightly extended-proptest job"]
322	    fn proptest_button_event_sequences_preserve_invariants(
323	        props in arb_button_props(),
324	        events in prop::collection::vec(arb_button_event(), 0..128),
325	    ) {
326	        let mut service = Service::<button::Machine>::new(
327	            props,
328	            &Env::default(),
329	            &button::Messages::default(),
330	        );
331	
332	        for event in events {
333	            drop(service.send(event));
334	
335	            let state = service.state();
336	            let ctx = service.context();
337	
338	            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.loading, matches!(state, button::State::Loading));
339	            prop_assert_eq!(ctx.pressed, matches!(state, button::State::Pressed));
340	            prop_assert!(
341	                !ctx.focus_visible || ctx.focused,
342	                "focus-visible cannot outlive focus"
343	            );
344	
345	            if ctx.disabled {
346	                prop_assert!(!ctx.focused, "disabled button cannot stay focused");
347	                prop_assert!(
348	                    !ctx.focus_visible,
349	                    "disabled button cannot show focus-visible"
350	                );
351	                prop_assert!(!ctx.pressed, "disabled button cannot stay pressed");
352	            }
353	
354	            if ctx.loading {
355	                prop_assert!(!ctx.pressed, "loading button cannot stay pressed");
356	            }
357	
358	            match state {
359	                button::State::Idle => {
360	                    prop_assert!(!ctx.focused, "idle button cannot stay focused");
361	                    prop_assert!(
362	                        !ctx.focus_visible,
363	                        "idle button cannot show focus-visible"
364	                    );
365	                    prop_assert!(!ctx.pressed, "idle button cannot stay pressed");
366	                }
367	
368	                button::State::Focused => {
369	                    prop_assert!(ctx.focused, "focused state requires focused context");
370	                    prop_assert!(!ctx.pressed, "focused button cannot stay pressed");
371	                }
372	
373	                button::State::Pressed => {
374	                    prop_assert!(ctx.pressed, "pressed state requires pressed context");
375	                    prop_assert!(!ctx.loading, "pressed button cannot be loading");
376	                }
377	
378	                button::State::Loading => {
379	                    prop_assert!(ctx.loading, "loading state requires loading context");
380	                    prop_assert!(!ctx.pressed, "loading button cannot be pressed");
381	                }
382	            }
383	        }
384	    }

1	//! Live-region component state machine and connect API.
2	
3	use alloc::{
4	    string::{String, ToString},
5	    vec::Vec,
6	};
7	use core::{
8	    fmt::{self, Debug},
9	    time::Duration,
10	};
11	
12	use ars_a11y::AriaRelevant;
13	use ars_core::{
14	    AriaAttr, AttrMap, ComponentPart, ConnectApi, Env, HtmlAttr, PendingEffect, TransitionPlan,
15	};
16	
17	/// The state of the `LiveRegion` component.
18	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
19	pub enum State {
20	    /// No announcement is pending.
21	    Idle,
22	
23	    /// A message is moving through the clear-then-insert announcement cycle.
24	    Announcing,
25	}
26	
27	/// The events of the `LiveRegion` component.
28	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
29	pub enum Event {
30	    /// Queue a new announcement.
31	    Announce {
32	        /// The message to announce.
33	        message: String,
34	
35	        /// The announcement priority.
36	        priority: AnnouncePriority,
37	    },
38	
39	    /// Clear all pending and rendered announcements.
40	    Clear,
41	
42	    /// Signal that the adapter has completed one clear-then-insert cycle.
43	    Rendered,
44	
45	    /// Synchronize output-affecting props into context.
46	    SetProps,
47	}
48	
49	/// The politeness levels for `aria-live`.
50	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
51	pub enum AriaPoliteness {
52	    /// Do not announce changes.
53	    Off,
54	
55	    /// Announce changes politely after the current screen-reader output.
56	    Polite,
57	
58	    /// Announce changes assertively, interrupting current output when needed.
59	    Assertive,
60	}
61	
62	/// The priority levels for announcements.
63	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
64	pub enum AnnouncePriority {
65	    /// Use the component's configured live-region politeness.
66	    Normal,
67	
68	    /// Force an assertive live-region announcement.
69	    Urgent,
70	}
71	
72	/// Queued announcement data waiting for an active announcement cycle to finish.
73	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
74	pub struct QueuedAnnouncement {
75	    /// The message to announce.
76	    pub message: String,
77	
78	    /// The announcement priority.
79	    pub priority: AnnouncePriority,
80	
81	    /// Monotonic insertion order used to preserve FIFO ordering per priority.
82	    pub sequence: u64,
83	}
84	
85	/// The context of the `LiveRegion` component.
86	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
87	pub struct Context {
88	    /// The messages currently rendered inside the live region.
89	    pub messages: Vec<String>,
90	
91	    /// Messages waiting behind the active announcement.
92	    pub queue: Vec<QueuedAnnouncement>,
93	
94	    /// The base politeness of the live region.
95	    pub politeness: AriaPoliteness,
96	
97	    /// Whether the whole live region is announced for each update.
98	    pub atomic: bool,
99	
100	    /// The relevant changes for `aria-relevant`.

461	fn clear_plan(target: State) -> TransitionPlan<Machine> {
462	    TransitionPlan::to(target)
463	        .apply(|ctx: &mut Context| {
464	            ctx.messages.clear();
465	            ctx.queue.clear();
466	            ctx.pending_message = None;
467	            ctx.current_priority = AnnouncePriority::Normal;
468	        })
469	        .cancel_effect(Effect::AnnounceDelay)
470	}
471	
472	const fn has_announcements(ctx: &Context) -> bool {
473	    !ctx.messages.is_empty() || !ctx.queue.is_empty() || ctx.pending_message.is_some()
474	}
475	
476	fn announce_delay_effect() -> PendingEffect<Machine> {
477	    PendingEffect::named(Effect::AnnounceDelay)
478	}
479	
480	fn next_sequence(queue: &[QueuedAnnouncement]) -> u64 {
481	    queue
482	        .iter()
483	        .map(|announcement| announcement.sequence)
484	        .max()
485	        .map_or(0, |sequence| sequence.saturating_add(1))
486	}
487	
488	fn dequeue_next(queue: &mut Vec<QueuedAnnouncement>) -> Option<QueuedAnnouncement> {
489	    let index = queue
490	        .iter()
491	        .enumerate()
492	        .min_by_key(|(_, announcement)| {
493	            (
494	                priority_sort_key(announcement.priority),
495	                announcement.sequence,
496	            )
497	        })
498	        .map(|(index, _)| index)?;
499	
500	    Some(queue.remove(index))
501	}
502	
503	const fn priority_sort_key(priority: AnnouncePriority) -> u8 {
504	    match priority {
505	        AnnouncePriority::Urgent => 0,
506	        AnnouncePriority::Normal => 1,
507	    }
508	}
509	
510	const fn politeness_value(politeness: AriaPoliteness) -> &'static str {
511	    match politeness {
512	        AriaPoliteness::Off => "off",
513	        AriaPoliteness::Polite => "polite",
514	        AriaPoliteness::Assertive => "assertive",
515	    }
516	}

194	#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
195	pub enum Effect {
196	    /// Adapter performs the delayed clear-then-insert render cycle.
197	    AnnounceDelay,
198	}
199	
200	/// The machine for the `LiveRegion` component.
201	#[derive(Debug)]
202	pub struct Machine;
203	
204	impl ars_core::Machine for Machine {
205	    type State = State;
206	    type Event = Event;
207	    type Context = Context;
208	    type Props = Props;
209	    type Messages = Messages;
210	    type Effect = Effect;
211	    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;
212	
213	    fn init(
214	        props: &Self::Props,
215	        _env: &Env,
216	        _messages: &Self::Messages,
217	    ) -> (Self::State, Self::Context) {
218	        (
219	            State::Idle,
220	            Context {
221	                messages: Vec::new(),
222	                queue: Vec::new(),
223	                politeness: props.politeness,
224	                atomic: props.atomic,
225	                relevant: props.relevant.clone(),
226	                delay: props.delay,
227	                pending_message: None,
228	                current_priority: AnnouncePriority::Normal,
229	            },
230	        )
231	    }
232	
233	    fn transition(
234	        state: &Self::State,
235	        event: &Self::Event,
236	        ctx: &Self::Context,
237	        props: &Self::Props,
238	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
239	        match (state, event) {
240	            (State::Idle, Event::Announce { message, priority }) => {
241	                let message = message.clone();
242	                let priority = *priority;
243	
244	                Some(
245	                    TransitionPlan::to(State::Announcing)
246	                        .apply(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
247	                            ctx.messages.clear();
248	                            ctx.pending_message = Some(message);
249	                            ctx.current_priority = priority;
250	                        })
251	                        .with_effect(announce_delay_effect()),
252	                )
253	            }
254	
255	            (State::Announcing, Event::Announce { message, priority }) => {
256	                let message = message.clone();
257	                let priority = *priority;
258	
259	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
260	                    let sequence = next_sequence(&ctx.queue);
261	                    ctx.queue.push(QueuedAnnouncement {
262	                        message,
263	                        priority,
264	                        sequence,
265	                    });
266	                }))
267	            }
268	
269	            (State::Announcing, Event::Rendered) => Some(rendered_plan(!ctx.queue.is_empty())),
270	
271	            (_, Event::SetProps) => {
272	                let politeness = props.politeness;
273	                let atomic = props.atomic;
274	                let relevant = props.relevant.clone();
275	                let delay = props.delay;
276	
277	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(move |ctx: &mut Context| {
278	                    ctx.politeness = politeness;
279	                    ctx.atomic = atomic;
280	                    ctx.relevant = relevant;
281	                    ctx.delay = delay;
282	                }))
283	            }
284	
285	            (_, Event::Clear) if has_announcements(ctx) => Some(clear_plan(State::Idle)),
286	
287	            (State::Announcing, Event::Clear) => Some(clear_plan(State::Idle)),
288	
289	            (State::Idle, Event::Clear | Event::Rendered) => None,
290	        }
291	    }
292	
293	    fn on_props_changed(old: &Self::Props, new: &Self::Props) -> Vec<Self::Event> {
294	        assert_eq!(
295	            old.id, new.id,
296	            "live_region::Props.id must remain stable after init"
297	        );
298	
299	        if old.politeness != new.politeness

202	    fn init(props: &Props, _env: &Env, _messages: &Messages) -> (State, Context) {

204	            State::Inactive,
205	            Context {
206	                saved_focus: None,
207	                container_id: None,
208	            },
209	        )
210	    }
211	
212	    fn transition(
213	        state: &Self::State,
214	        event: &Self::Event,
215	        ctx: &Self::Context,
216	        props: &Self::Props,
217	    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {
218	        match (state, event) {
219	            // ── Activation ──────────────────────────────────────────────
220	            (State::Inactive, Event::Activate { trapped, saved_focus_id }) => {
221	                let trap = *trapped || props.contain;
222	                let auto_focus = props.auto_focus;
223	                let saved = saved_focus_id.clone();
224	                let mut plan = TransitionPlan::to(State::Active { trapped: trap })
225	                    .apply(move |ctx| {
226	                        ctx.saved_focus = saved;
227	                    })
228	                    .with_named_effect("focus-trap-listener", |ctx, _props, send| {
229	                        let platform = use_platform_effects();
230	                        let container_id = ctx.ids.part("container");
231	                        platform.attach_focus_trap(&container_id, Box::new(move || {
232	                            send.call_if_alive(Event::Deactivate { restore_focus: true });
233	                        }))
234	                    });
235	                if auto_focus {
236	                    plan = plan.then(Event::FocusFirst);
237	                }
238	                Some(plan)
239	            }
240	
241	            // ── Deactivation ────────────────────────────────────────────
242	            (State::Active { .. }, Event::Deactivate { restore_focus }) => {
243	                let restore = *restore_focus;
244	                let mut plan = TransitionPlan::to(State::Inactive);
245	                if restore {
246	                    // RestoreFocus will read saved_focus then clear it.
247	                    plan = plan.then(Event::RestoreFocus);
248	                } else {
249	                    plan = plan.apply(|ctx| {
250	                        ctx.saved_focus = None; // Clear to prevent stale DOM references
251	                    });
252	                }
253	                Some(plan)
254	            }
255	
256	            // ── Trap / Release ──────────────────────────────────────────
257	            (State::Active { trapped: false }, Event::TrapFocus) => {
258	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Active { trapped: true }))
259	            }
260	            (State::Active { trapped: true }, Event::ReleaseTrap) => {
261	                Some(TransitionPlan::to(State::Active { trapped: false }))
262	            }
263	
264	            // ── RestoreFocus ────────────────────────────────────────────
265	            // Only processed when Inactive (after Deactivate via then_send).
266	            (State::Inactive, Event::RestoreFocus) => {
267	                Some(TransitionPlan::context_only(|ctx| {
268	                    if let Some(ref target) = ctx.saved_focus {
269	                        restore_focus_safely(target.clone(), &[]);
270	                    }
271	                    ctx.saved_focus = None;
272	                }))
273	            }
274	            // If RestoreFocus arrives while Active, ignore it.
275	            (State::Active { .. }, Event::RestoreFocus) => None,
276	
277	            // ── Focus Navigation ────────────────────────────────────────
278	            (State::Active { .. }, Event::FocusFirst) => {
279	                Some(TransitionPlan::new()
280	                    .with_named_effect("focus_first", |ctx, _props, _send| {
281	                        let platform = use_platform_effects();
282	                        if let Some(ref id) = ctx.container_id {
283	                            platform.focus_first_tabbable(id);
284	                        }
285	                    }))
286	            }
287	            (State::Active { .. }, Event::FocusLast) => {
288	                Some(TransitionPlan::new()
289	                    .with_named_effect("focus_last", |ctx, _props, _send| {
290	                        let platform = use_platform_effects();
291	                        if let Some(ref id) = ctx.container_id {
292	                            platform.focus_last_tabbable(id);
293	                        }
294	                    }))
295	            }
296	
297	            _ => None,
298	        }
299	    }
300	
301	    // **Modality coordination:** programmatic focus restoration MUST preserve the
302	    // shared `ModalityContext` state rather than forcing pointer modality.
303	    // This ensures `data-ars-focus-visible` remains correct — programmatic focus
304	    // should only show a focus ring when the prior interaction was not pointer-driven.
305	
306	    fn connect<'a>(
307	        state: &'a Self::State,
308	        ctx: &'a Self::Context,
309	        props: &'a Self::Props,
310	        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),
311	    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {
312	        Api { state, ctx, props, send }
313	    }
314	}
315	```

317	#### 1.6.1 Focus Restoration Safety
318	
319	````rust
320	// Guard against restoring focus to a removed or unfocusable element.
321	//
322	// Checks performed before restoring focus:
323	// 1. Element is in the DOM (document.contains)
324	// 2. Element is visible (not visibility:hidden, not display:none)
325	// 3. Element is not inside a closed <details> element
326	// 4. Element is not already the active element (document.activeElement)
327	// 5. Element can receive focus (is tabbable or has tabindex)
328	// 6. Element has layout (offsetParent != null)
329	//
330	// If the target fails any check, try each fallback in order, then the
331	// nearest focusable ancestor, then the document body.
332	//
333	// The `fallbacks` parameter supports nested dialog scenarios where the
334	// original trigger may have been removed. Callers pass a prioritized list
335	// (e.g., [parent_dialog_last_focused, parent_dialog_container,
336	// parent_dialog_first_focusable]) so the function can gracefully degrade.
337	fn restore_focus_safely(target_id: &str, fallback_ids: &[&str]) {
338	    let platform = use_platform_effects();
339	    if platform.can_restore_focus(target_id) {
340	        platform.focus_element_by_id(target_id);
341	        return;
342	    }
343	    // Try fallback elements in order
344	    for id in fallback_ids {
345	        if platform.can_restore_focus(id) {
346	            platform.focus_element_by_id(id);
347	            return;
348	        }
349	    }
350	    // Walk up to nearest focusable ancestor of the original target.
351	    if let Some(ancestor_id) = platform.nearest_focusable_ancestor_id(target_id) {
352	        platform.focus_element_by_id(&ancestor_id);
353	    } else {
354	        // Last resort — focus document body
355	        platform.focus_body();
356	    }
357	}
358	
359	// ── Orientation Change Focus Audit ──────────────────────────────────────
360	//
361	// When the viewport orientation changes (e.g., portrait → landscape on mobile),
362	// CSS media queries may hide or show elements. If FocusScope has trapped focus
363	// on an element that is now hidden by `display: none`, the element stays in
364	// the DOM but is invisible and has `offsetParent == null`.
365	//
366	// The FocusScope Tab handler MUST check `offsetParent !== null` before allowing
367	// focus on any element. Additionally, the adapter MUST register a
368	// `matchMedia('(orientation: portrait)')` change listener that triggers a
369	// focus audit when orientation changes:
370	//
371	//   1. Check if the currently focused element has `offsetParent == null`.
372	//   2. If hidden, move focus to the first visible tabbable element in the scope.
373	//   3. Update the saved_focus reference if the restore target is now hidden.
374	//
375	// ```rust
376	// fn audit_focus_on_orientation_change(scope: &FocusScopeContext) {
377	//     let platform = use_platform_effects();
378	//     if let Some(active_id) = platform.active_element_id() {
379	//         if !platform.has_layout(&active_id) {
380	//             // Focused element is hidden — move to first visible tabbable
381	//             if let Some(ref container_id) = scope.container_id {
382	//                 platform.focus_first_tabbable(container_id);
383	//             }
384	//         }
385	//     }
386	//     // Also validate the saved_focus restore target
387	//     if let Some(ref saved) = scope.saved_focus {
388	//         if !platform.has_layout(saved) {
389	//             // Restore target is now hidden; clear it so fallback chain is used
390	//             scope.saved_focus = None;
391	//         }
392	//     }
393	// }
394	// ```

397	### 1.7 Connect / API
398	
399	```rust
400	#[derive(ComponentPart)]
401	#[scope = "focus-scope"]
402	pub enum Part {
403	    Container,
404	}
405	
406	/// The API for the `FocusScope` component.
407	pub struct Api<'a> {
408	    /// The current state of the focus scope.
409	    state: &'a State,
410	    /// The context of the focus scope.
411	    ctx: &'a Context,
412	    /// The props of the focus scope.
413	    props: &'a Props,
414	    /// The send function for the focus scope.
415	    send: &'a dyn Fn(Event),
416	}
417	
418	impl<'a> Api<'a> {
419	    /// Whether the focus scope is active.
420	    pub fn is_active(&self) -> bool {
421	        matches!(self.state, State::Active { .. })
422	    }
423	
424	    /// Whether the focus scope is trapped.
425	    pub fn is_trapped(&self) -> bool {
426	        matches!(self.state, State::Active { trapped: true })
427	    }
428	
429	    /// Props for the container element that scopes focus.
430	    pub fn container_attrs(&self) -> AttrMap {
431	        let mut attrs = AttrMap::new();
432	        let [(scope_attr, scope_val), (part_attr, part_val)] = Part::Container.data_attrs();
433	        attrs.set(scope_attr, scope_val);
434	        attrs.set(part_attr, part_val);
435	        if self.is_active() {
436	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-active"), true);
437	
438	            // tabindex="-1" allows the container itself to be focused programmatically,
439	            // which is needed as a focus target when no tabbable children exist yet.
440	            // Only set when active — an inactive scope's container should not appear
441	            // as a programmatic focus target to screen readers.
442	            attrs.set(HtmlAttr::TabIndex, "-1");
443	        }
444	        if self.is_trapped() {
445	            attrs.set_bool(HtmlAttr::Data("ars-trapped"), true);
446	        }
447	        // Event handlers (keydown for Tab trapping) are typed methods on the Api struct.
448	        attrs
449	    }
450	
451	    /// Imperatively activate the focus scope.
452	    /// `saved_focus_id` is the ID of the currently focused element, captured by
453	    /// the adapter via `platform.active_element_id()` before calling this.
454	    pub fn activate(&self, trapped: bool, saved_focus_id: Option<String>) {
455	        (self.send)(Event::Activate { trapped, saved_focus_id });
456	    }
457	
458	    /// Imperatively deactivate the focus scope.
459	    pub fn deactivate(&self, restore_focus: bool) {
460	        (self.send)(Event::Deactivate { restore_focus });
461	    }
462	
463	    /// Move focus to the first tabbable element within the container.
464	    /// Used by framework adapters when auto_focus=true.
465	    pub fn focus_first(&self) {
466	        let platform = use_platform_effects();
467	        if let Some(ref id) = self.ctx.container_id {
468	            platform.focus_first_tabbable(id);
469	        }
470	    }
471	
472	    /// Move focus to the last tabbable element within the container.
473	    pub fn focus_last(&self) {
474	        let platform = use_platform_effects();
475	        if let Some(ref id) = self.ctx.container_id {
476	            platform.focus_last_tabbable(id);
477	        }
478	    }
479	
480	    /// Return IDs of all currently tabbable elements in the container.
481	    pub fn get_tabbable_elements(&self) -> Vec<String> {
482	        let platform = use_platform_effects();
483	        self.ctx.container_id
484	            .as_ref()
485	            .map(|id| platform.tabbable_element_ids(id))
486	            .unwrap_or_default()
487	    }
488	
489	    /// Move focus to the next focusable element within the scope.
490	    /// Returns `true` if focus was moved, `false` if no valid target exists.
491	    /// Used by Toolbar, ActionGroup, and TreeView for programmatic sequential
492	    /// focus movement beyond roving tabindex.
493	    pub fn focus_next(&self, _opts: FocusNavigationOptions) -> bool {
494	        // IMPL: traverse tabbable elements forward within scope
495	        false
496	    }
497	
498	    /// Move focus to the previous focusable element within the scope.
499	    /// Returns `true` if focus was moved, `false` if no valid target exists.
500	    pub fn focus_previous(&self, _opts: FocusNavigationOptions) -> bool {
501	        // IMPL: traverse tabbable elements backward within scope
502	        false
503	    }
504	}
505	
506	impl ConnectApi for Api<'_> {
507	    type Part = Part;
508	
509	    fn part_attrs(&self, part: Part) -> AttrMap {
510	        match part {
511	            Part::Container => self.container_attrs(),
512	        }
513	    }
514	}
515	
516	/// Options for focus navigation.
517	#[derive(Clone)]
518	pub struct FocusNavigationOptions {
519	    /// Wrap around at boundaries.
520	    pub wrap: bool,
521	    /// Element ID to start from (default: currently focused element).
522	    pub from: Option<String>,
523	    /// Only consider tabbable elements (tabindex >= 0).
524	    pub tabbable: bool,
525	    /// Custom filter predicate that receives an element ID.
526	    /// Uses `Rc` so the options struct can be cloned.
527	    pub accept: Option<Rc<dyn Fn(&str) -> bool>>,
528	}

`.cancel_effect(Effect::FocusTrapListener)` on `Deactivate`.\n    FocusTrapListener,\n    /// Move focus to the first tabbable descendant of the container.\n    /// Fall back to focusing the container itself when none exist.\n    FocusFirst,\n    /// Move focus to the last tabbable descendant of the container.\n    /// Fall back to focusing the container itself when none exist.\n    FocusLast,\n    /// Restore focus to `service.context().saved_focus`, applying the §7\n    /// fallback chain when the saved target is no longer focusable.\n    RestoreFocus,\n}\n\nimpl ars_core::Machine for Machine {\n    type State = State;\n    type Event = Event;\n    type Context = Context;\n    type Props = Props;\n    type Messages = Messages;\n    type Effect = Effect;\n    type Api<'a> = Api<'a>;\n\n    fn init(_props: &Props, _env: &Env, _messages: &Messages) -> (State, Context) {\n        (\n            State::Inactive,\n            Context {\n                saved_focus: None,\n                container_id: None,\n            },\n        )\n    }\n\n    fn transition(\n        state: &Self::State,\n        event: &Self::Event,\n        _ctx: &Self::Context,\n        props: &Self::Props,\n    ) -> Option<TransitionPlan<Self>> {\n        match (state, event) {\n            // ── Activation ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n            (State::Inactive, Event::Activate { trapped, saved_focus_id }) => {\n                let trap = *trapped || props.contain;\n                let auto_focus = props.auto_focus;\n                let saved = saved_focus_id.clone();\n })\n                    .with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusTrapListener));\n                if auto_focus {\n                    plan = plan.then(Event::FocusFirst);\n                }\n             let restore = *restore_focus;\n                let mut plan = TransitionPlan::to(State::Inactive)\n                    .cancel_effect(Effect::FocusTrapListener);\n                if When `Inactive` we emit the effect intent;\n            // when `Active` the event is ignored — restoration is only\n            // meaningful after the scope has deactivated.\n            (State::Inactive, Event::RestoreFocus) => Some(\n                TransitionPlan::new().with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::RestoreFocus)),\n            ),\n            (State::Active { .. }, Event::RestoreFocus) => None,\n\n            // ── Focus Navigation ────────────────────────────────────────\n            (State::Active { .. }, Event::FocusFirst) => Some(\n                TransitionPlan::new().with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusFirst)),\n            ),\n            (State::Active { .. }, Event::FocusLast) => Some(\n                TransitionPlan::new().with_effect(PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusLast)),\n            ),\n\n            _ => None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    // **Modality coordination:** programmatic focus restoration MUST preserve the\n    // shared `ModalityContext` state rather than forcing pointer modality.\n    // This ensures `data-ars-focus-visible` remains correct — programmatic focus\n    // should only show a focus ring when the prior interaction was not pointer-driven.\n\n    fn connect<'a>(\n        state: &'a Self::State,\n        ctx: &'a Self::Context,\n        props: &'a Self::Props,\n        send: &'a dyn Fn(Self::Event),\n    ) -> Self::Api<'a> {\n        Api { state, ctx, props, send }\n    }\n}\n```"

  "old_string": "#### 1.6.1 Focus Restoration Safety\n\n````rust\n// Guard against restoring focus to a removed or unfocusable element.\n//\n// Checks performed before restoring focus:\n// 1. Element is in the DOM (document.contains)\n// 2. Element is visible (not visibility:hidden, not display:none)\n// 3. Element is not inside a closed <details> element\n// 4. Element is not already the active element (document.activeElement)\n// 5. Element can receive focus (is tabbable or has tabindex)\n// 6. Element has layout (offsetParent != null)\n//\n// If the target fails any check, try each fallback in order, then the\n// nearest focusable ancestor, then the document body.\n//\n// The `fallbacks` parameter supports nested dialog scenarios where the\n// original trigger may have been removed. Callers pass a prioritized list\n// (e.g., [parent_dialog_last_focused, parent_dialog_container,\n// parent_dialog_first_focusable]) so the function can gracefully degrade.\nfn restore_focus_safely(target_id: &str, fallback_ids: &[&str]) {\n    let platform = use_platform_effects();\n    if platform.can_restore_focus(target_id) {\n        platform.focus_element_by_id(target_id);\n        return;\n    }\n    // Try fallback elements in order\n    for id in fallback_ids {\n        if platform.can_restore_focus(id) {\n            platform.focus_element_by_id(id);\n            return;\n        }\n    }\n    // Walk up to nearest focusable ancestor of the original target.\n    if let Some(ancestor_id) = platform.nearest_focusable_ancestor_id(target_id) {\n        platform.focus_element_by_id(&ancestor_id);\n    } else {\n        // Last resort — focus document body\n        platform.focus_body();\n    }\n}\n\n// ── Orientation Change Focus Audit ──────────────────────────────────────\n//\n// When the viewport orientation changes (e.g., portrait → landscape on mobile),\n// CSS media queries may hide or show elements. If FocusScope has trapped focus\n// on an element that is now hidden by `display: none`, the element stays in\n// the DOM but is invisible and has `offsetParent == null`.\n//\n// The FocusScope Tab handler MUST check `offsetParent !== null` before allowing\n// focus on any element. Additionally, the adapter MUST register a\n// `matchMedia('(orientation: portrait)')` change listener that triggers a\n// focus audit when orientation changes:\n//\n//   1. Check if the currently focused element has `offsetParent == null`.\n//   2. If hidden, move focus to the first visible tabbable element in the scope.\n//   3. Update the saved_focus reference if the restore target is now hidden.\n//\n// ```rust\n// fn audit_focus_on_orientation_change(scope: &FocusScopeContext) {\n//     let platform = use_platform_effects();\n//     if let Some(active_id) = platform.active_element_id() {\n//         if !platform.has_layout(&active_id) {\n//             // Focused element is hidden — move to first visible tabbable\n//             if let Some(ref container_id) = scope.container_id {\n//                 platform.focus_first_tabbable(container_id);\n//             }\n//         }\n//     }\n//     // Also validate the saved_focus restore target\n//     if let Some(ref saved) = scope.saved_focus {\n//         if !platform.has_layout(saved) {\n//             // Restore target is now hidden; clear it so fallback chain is used\n//             scope.saved_focus = None;\n//         }\n//     }\n// }\n// ```\n````",
  "new_string": "#### 1.6.1 Focus Restoration Safety (adapter contract)\n\nThe agnostic core stores the saved focus target as an opaque `Option<String>`\non `Context::saved_focus`. When `Effect::RestoreFocus` fires, the adapter MUST\nguard against restoring focus to a removed, hidden, or otherwise unfocusable\nelement by walking the following safety checks before calling\n`PlatformEffects::focus_element_by_id`:\n\n1. The element is connected to the DOM (`document.contains` returns true).\n2. The element is visible (`visibility` is not `hidden`; `display` is not\n   `none`).\n3. The element is not inside a closed `<details>` element.\n4. The element is not already the active element (avoid a no-op refocus).\n5. The element can receive focus (tabbable or has explicit `tabindex`).\n6. The element has layout (`offsetParent !== null`).\n\nEach predicate above corresponds to a single `PlatformEffects::can_restore_focus`\ncall — adapters typically implement the entire check list inside that one\nmethod. If the saved target fails any check, the adapter applies the fallback\nchain documented in §7.\n\n#### 1.6.2 Orientation-Change Focus Audit (adapter contract)\n\nWhen viewport orientation changes (portrait ↔ landscape on mobile), CSS media\nqueries can hide or show elements without removing them from the DOM. If the\ncurrently focused element is hidden by `display: none` after the change, it\nstill has focus but `offsetParent` becomes `null`. The agnostic core does not\nobserve orientation events — adapters that need this behavior MUST register a\n`matchMedia('(orientation: portrait)')` change listener that:\n\n1. Reads the currently focused element via\n   `PlatformEffects::active_element_id`.\n2. If the focused element lacks layout (per the §1.6.1 check list), emits a\n   fresh `Event::FocusFirst` to move focus to the first visible tabbable in\n   the scope.\n3. If `service.context().saved_focus` references an element that lacks layout\n   after the change, the adapter may inform the consumer that the restore\n   target is no longer valid — the core's fallback chain (§7) handles the\n   actual recovery on the next `Effect::RestoreFocus` dispatch."
}
```

  "old_string": "    /// Move focus to the first tabbable element within the container.\n    /// Used by framework adapters when auto_focus=true.\n    pub fn focus_first(&self) {\n        let platform = use_platform_effects();\n        if let Some(ref id) = self.ctx.container_id {\n            platform.focus_first_tabbable(id);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Move focus to the last tabbable element within the container.\n    pub fn focus_last(&self) {\n        let platform = use_platform_effects();\n        if let Some(ref id) = self.ctx.container_id {\n            platform.focus_last_tabbable(id);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Return IDs of all currently tabbable elements in the container.\n    pub fn get_tabbable_elements(&self) -> Vec<String> {\n        let platform = use_platform_effects();\n        self.ctx.container_id\n            .as_ref()\n            .map(|id| platform.tabbable_element_ids(id))\n            .unwrap_or_default()\n    }\n\n    /// Move focus to the next focusable element within the scope.\n    /// Returns `true` if focus was moved, `false` if no valid target exists.\n    /// Used by Toolbar, ActionGroup, and TreeView for programmatic sequential\n    /// focus movement beyond roving tabindex.\n    pub fn focus_next(&self, _opts: FocusNavigationOptions) -> bool {\n        // IMPL: traverse tabbable elements forward within scope\n        false\n    }\n\n    /// Move focus to the previous focusable element within the scope.\n    /// Returns `true` if focus was moved, `false` if no valid target exists.\n    pub fn focus_previous(&self, _opts: FocusNavigationOptions) -> bool {\n        // IMPL: traverse tabbable elements backward within scope\n        false\n    }\n}",
  "new_string": "    /// Request that focus move to the first tabbable descendant of the\n    /// container. Sends [`Event::FocusFirst`]; the agnostic core then emits\n    /// [`Effect::FocusFirst`] which the adapter routes through\n    /// `PlatformEffects::focus_first_tabbable`. Used by framework adapters\n    /// when `auto_focus=true`.\n    pub fn focus_first(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::FocusFirst);\n    }\n\n    /// Request that focus move to the last tabbable descendant of the\n    /// container. Sends [`Event::FocusLast`].\n    pub fn focus_last(&self) {\n        (self.send)(Event::FocusLast);\n    }\n\n    /// Move focus to the next focusable element within the scope.\n    ///\n    /// Returns `true` if focus was moved, `false` if no valid target exists.\n    /// The agnostic core cannot resolve tabbable elements synchronously — it\n    /// returns `false` here as a stable contract. Adapters layer a\n    /// platform-aware variant on top (consumed by `Toolbar`, `ActionGroup`,\n    /// and `TreeView` for programmatic sequential focus movement beyond\n    /// roving tabindex).\n    pub fn focus_next(&self, _opts: FocusNavigationOptions) -> bool {\n        false\n    }\n\n    /// Move focus to the previous focusable element within the scope.\n    ///\n    /// Returns `true` if focus was moved, `false` if no valid target exists.\n    /// Adapter-overridable; see [`focus_next`](Self::focus_next).\n    pub fn focus_previous(&self, _opts: FocusNavigationOptions) -> bool {\n        false\n    }\n}"
}

  "old_string": "/// Options for focus navigation.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct FocusNavigationOptions {\n    /// Wrap around at boundaries.\n    pub wrap: bool,\n    /// Element ID to start from (default: currently focused element).\n    pub from: Option<String>,\n    /// Only consider tabbable elements (tabindex >= 0).\n    pub tabbable: bool,\n    /// Custom filter predicate that receives an element ID.\n    /// Uses `Rc` so the options struct can be cloned.\n    pub accept: Option<Rc<dyn Fn(&str) -> bool>>,\n}\n```",
  "new_string": "/// Options for focus navigation.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct FocusNavigationOptions {\n    /// Wrap around at boundaries.\n    pub wrap: bool,\n    /// Element ID to start from (default: currently focused element).\n    pub from: Option<String>,\n    /// Only consider tabbable elements (tabindex >= 0).\n    pub tabbable: bool,\n    /// Custom filter predicate that receives an element ID.\n    /// Uses `Rc` so the options struct can be cloned.\n    pub accept: Option<Rc<dyn Fn(&str) -> bool>>,\n}\n```\n\n### 1.8 Effect Contract\n\nAdapters MUST provide a handler for every [`Effect`] variant. Each handler\nreceives `&Context`, `&Props`, and a `WeakSend<Event>` send handle, and is\nexpected to call into the workspace's `PlatformEffects` implementation\n(see `spec/foundation/01-architecture.md` §2.2.7 and\n`spec/foundation/11-dom-utilities.md` §3) rather than the DOM directly.\n\n| Effect              | When emitted                                                          | Adapter contract                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |\n| ------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `FocusTrapListener` | `Inactive → Active` (Activate)                                        | Call `PlatformEffects::attach_focus_trap(container_id, on_escape)`. Wire the returned `CleanupFn` as the effect's cleanup so `cancel_effect(Effect::FocusTrapListener)` tears the handler down on `Deactivate`. The `on_escape` callback SHOULD send `Event::Deactivate { restore_focus: true }`.        |\n| `FocusFirst`        | `Active + FocusFirst` (also on `Activate` when `props.auto_focus`)    | Call `PlatformEffects::focus_first_tabbable(container_id)`. Fall back to focusing the container element (which has `tabindex=\"-1\"` while active) when no tabbable descendants exist.                                                                                                                    |\n| `FocusLast`         | `Active + FocusLast`                                                  | Call `PlatformEffects::focus_last_tabbable(container_id)`. Fall back to the container when no tabbable descendants exist.                                                                                                                                                                                |\n| `RestoreFocus`      | `Active → Inactive` (Deactivate with `restore_focus: true`) or explicit `Event::RestoreFocus` while `Inactive` | Read `service.context().saved_focus`. If `Some(id)`, apply the §1.6.1 / §7 fallback chain via `PlatformEffects::can_restore_focus` → `focus_element_by_id` → `nearest_focusable_ancestor_id` → `focus_body`. The core does not clear `saved_focus` here; the next `Activate` overwrites it.            |"
}

```
137	### 1.5 Transitions
138	
139	```text
140	Inactive + Activate { trapped }
141	  → Active { trapped: trapped || contain }
142	  action: save currently focused element → ctx.saved_focus
143	  effect: "focus-trap-listener" (attaches keydown handler to intercept Tab)
144	  then_send: FocusFirst (if auto_focus=true)
145	
146	Active + Deactivate { restore_focus }
147	  → Inactive
148	  action: clear ctx.saved_focus reference
149	  cleanup effect: remove keydown handler
150	  then_send: RestoreFocus (if restore_focus=true)
151	
152	Active { trapped: false } + TrapFocus
153	  → Active { trapped: true }
154	
155	Active { trapped: true } + ReleaseTrap
156	  → Active { trapped: false }
157	
158	Inactive + RestoreFocus
159	  → Inactive (stay)
160	  action: restore focus from ctx.saved_focus via restore_focus_safely()
161	
162	Active + RestoreFocus
163	  → None (ignored — RestoreFocus is only meaningful after Deactivate)
164	
165	Active + FocusFirst
166	  → Active (stay)
167	  effect: focus first tabbable element in container
168	
169	Active + FocusLast
170	  → Active (stay)
171	  effect: focus last tabbable element in container
172	
173	When `contain` is true and no tabbable elements exist within the scope, FocusScope MUST:
174	  (1) keep focus on the container element (which has `tabindex="-1"`),
175	  (2) suppress Tab/Shift+Tab key events entirely (`preventDefault()`),
176	  (3) re-scan for tabbable elements on each Tab press to detect dynamically added
177	      content (e.g., lazy-loaded dialog body).
178	  (handled entirely in effect, no state change needed)
179	```
180	
181	### 1.6 Full Machine Implementation
182	
183	The agnostic core emits typed effect intents via `Effect` markers. Adapters
184	dispatch on the `Effect` variant and route to their `PlatformEffects`
185	implementation — the core never calls platform helpers directly. This matches
186	the named-effect pattern used by `Dialog` (`spec/components/overlay/dialog.md`)
```

  "new_string": "### 1.5 Transitions\n\n```text\nInactive + Activate { trapped, saved_focus_id }\n  → Active { trapped: trapped || props.contain }\n  action: ctx.saved_focus = saved_focus_id (adapter-captured ID)\n  effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusTrapListener)\n  then_send: FocusFirst (if props.auto_focus=true)\n\nActive + Deactivate { restore_focus }\n  → Inactive\n  cancel_effect: Effect::FocusTrapListener  (runs the adapter cleanup, tearing\n                                              down the Tab keydown handler)\n  if restore_focus:\n    effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::RestoreFocus)\n    (ctx.saved_focus stays — adapter reads it; next Activate overwrites)\n  else:\n    action: ctx.saved_focus = None\n\nActive { trapped: false } + TrapFocus\n  → Active { trapped: true }\n\nActive { trapped: true } + ReleaseTrap\n  → Active { trapped: false }\n\nInactive + RestoreFocus\n  → Inactive (stay)\n  effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::RestoreFocus)\n\nActive + RestoreFocus\n  → None (ignored — restoration is only meaningful after Deactivate)\n\nActive + FocusFirst\n  → Active (stay)\n  effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusFirst)\n\nActive + FocusLast\n  → Active (stay)\n  effect: PendingEffect::named(Effect::FocusLast)\n\nWhen `contain` is true and no tabbable elements exist within the scope, the\nadapter's `Effect::FocusTrapListener` handler MUST:\n  (1) keep focus on the container element (which has `tabindex=\"-1\"`),\n  (2) suppress Tab/Shift+Tab key events entirely (`preventDefault()`),\n  (3) re-scan for tabbable elements on each Tab press to detect dynamically\n      added content (e.g., lazy-loaded dialog body).\n  (handled entirely in the adapter effect — no state change needed)\n```"
}

53	/// The events for the `FocusScope` component.
54	#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
55	pub enum Event {
56	    /// Activate the focus scope, optionally trapping focus.
57	    /// The adapter captures the currently focused element ID before sending this event
58	    /// (via `platform.active_element_id()`) so the machine can store it for later restoration.
59	    Activate {
60	        /// When true, Tab cannot escape the container.
61	        trapped: bool,
62	        /// ID of the element that had focus before activation (for restore-on-deactivate).
63	        /// Captured by the adapter via `platform.active_element_id()`.
64	        saved_focus_id: Option<String>,
65	    },
66	    /// Deactivate the scope and optionally restore previous focus.
67	    Deactivate {
68	        /// When true, restore focus to the previously focused element.
69	        restore_focus: bool,
70	    },
71	    /// Enable focus trapping on an active scope.
72	    TrapFocus,
73	    /// Disable focus trapping on an active scope.
74	    ReleaseTrap,
75	    /// Restore focus to the element that was focused before activation.
76	    /// Only processed during deactivation (via `then_send` from Deactivate).
77	    /// Ignored if the scope is still Active.
78	    RestoreFocus,
79	    /// Move focus to the first tabbable element in the container.
80	    FocusFirst,
81	    /// Move focus to the last tabbable element in the container.
82	    FocusLast,
83	}
84	
85	// FocusScope props (`trapped`, `contain`, `auto_focus`, `restore_focus`) are read
86	// at activation time and are immutable during the active lifecycle. To change
87	// trapping behavior, deactivate and reactivate the scope.
```
